**And three *amohs* was its height.** The height of the base excluding the height of the wheel (meaning: excluding the base [=כן] itself; יד האופן is the wheel) upon which it rests was an *amoh* and a half, and the height of the base was an *amoh* and a half. (Four *amohs* was the length, etc.) The *amoh* of the height was square but the half *amoh* was round, as it is stated, “At the top of the base [was a frame] half an *amoh* high circular all around.”⁴⁷

footnotes:
⁴⁷ Below v. 35.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:28:1)


**מִסְגְּרֹת לָהֶם.** רָאִיתִי בַּמִּשְׁנָה אַרְבָּעִים וְתֵשַׁע מִדּוֹת, הַמִּסְגְּרוֹת כְּמִין טַבְלָאוֹת.


**They had frames.** I saw in the Mishnah of “Forty-Nine Measurements,” that the מסגרות were in the form of טבלאות.⁴⁸

footnotes:
⁴⁸ The מסגרות are “boards” or “panels” on each of the four sides, and they served as a frame for the base. Therefore, they are referred to as “מסגרות” which means frames, as in Shemos 25:25, 27.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:28:2)


**מִסְגְּרוֹת לָהֶם.** הַמִּסְגְּרוֹת מִיַּד אוֹפַן לְיַד אוֹפָן, וְיַד אוֹפַן, הוּא עֵץ הַתָּחוּב בְּתוֹךְ הָאוֹפַן בַּנֶּקֶב, וְקָרוּי איישי"ל בְּלַעַ"ז (צִיר שֶׁל גַּלְגַּל). וְאַרְבָּעָה הָאוֹפַנִּים בַּמְּכוֹנָה, מִסְגְּרוֹת מִכָּאן וּמִסְגְּרוֹת מִכָּאן, מֵאַרְבָּעָה צְדָדִין, וּבֵין זוֹ לָזוֹ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת רֹחַב, וּמִלְּמַעְלָה 'סְרָנִים', כְּמוֹ 'נְסָרִים ', מוּל הַמִּסְגְּרוֹת, וְהַשְּׁלַבִּים מִן הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה לָעֶלְיוֹנָה. וּמִסְגְּרוֹת אֲחֵרוֹת. בֵּין הַשְּׁלַבִּים. כְּעֵין מְלֶאכֶת כַּרְעֵי הַמִּטָּה שֶׁל בְּנֵי כְּפָר, שֶׁאֵינָן עֲגֻלִּים אֶלָּא בּוֹלֵט, לָצוּר בָּהֶם אֲרָיוֹת וּכְרוּבִים. וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי, שֶׁהַשְּׁלַבִּים הַלָּלוּ כְּעֵין שְׁלִיבוֹת סֻלָּם, כְּמִין מַקְלוֹת נְחשֶׁת זְקוּפִין עַל הַמִּסְגְּרוֹת הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה, כִּשְׁנַיִם וּשְׁלשָׁה בֵּין מַקֵּל לְמַקֵּל, מִסְגְּרוֹת קְבוּעוֹת מִשְּׁלִיבָה לִשְׁלִיבָה. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: שְׁלִיבַיָּא.


**They had frames.** The frames were from the axle of one wheel to the axle of the other wheel. And the “יד אופן” is the wood which is inserted into the wheel, in the hole, and it is called *essieu*, in O.F. And the four wheels of the base had frames on this side and frames on the other side, on all four sides; between one and the other, was a width of four *amohs*, and above there were “blocks [=סרנים which means] the same as “boards” [=נסרים], against the frames, and the ledges were from the lower [frames] to the upper ones, and there were other frames between the ledges, like the design of the legs of the bed of villagers which are not round but protrude [in order] to decorate them with lions and cherubim. But I say, that these ledges were like the rungs of a ladder, sort of copper rods standing on the lower frames, there were two or three rods. The frames were set between one rod and the other, connected from one ledge to the other. [Targum] Yonoson, also, rendered “ledges.”



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:29:1)


**וְעַל הַמִּסְגְּרוֹת אֲשֶׁר בֵּין הַשְּׁלַבִּים.** הָיוּ מְצֻיָּרוֹת צוּרוֹת אֲרָיוֹת, צוּרוֹת בְּקָרִים וּכְרוּבִים.


**And on the frames that were between the rungs.** Were decorated with forms of lions, oxen and cherubim.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:29:2)


**וְעַל הַשְּׁלַבִּים כֵּן.** בָּסִיס, לְהוֹשִׁיב הַכִּיּוֹר עָלָיו, מִלְּמַעְלָה לַמִּסְגֶּרֶת אֲשֶׁר עַל רֹאשָׁם.


**And on the rungs there was a base.** A base, to place the basin upon it, above the frame which was on their top.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:29:3)


**וּמִתַּחַת לַאֲרָיוֹת וְלַבָּקָר.** הַמְרֻקָּמִין עַל הַמִּסְגְּרוֹת שֶׁבֵּין הַשְּׁלַבִּים.


**And beneath the lions and oxen.** Which were embroidered or attached on the outer frames which were between the ledges.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:29:4)


**לֹיוֹת מַעֲשֵׂה מוֹרָד.** לֹיוֹת, כְּמִין זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה מְחֻבָּרִין זֶה בָּזֶה.


**Joined together.** לויות is form resembling a male and female embracing.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:29:5)


**מַעֲשֵׂה מוֹרָד.** מְרֻדָּדִין, כְּמִין רִדּוּד טַס דַּק, וְאֵין בּוֹלְטִין בְּעָבְיוֹ, וְלֹא חֲקוּקִים בְּשִׁקּוּעַ. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם לֹיוֹת מַעֲשֵׂה מוֹרָד: מַדְבַּק עוֹבַד כִּבּוּשׁ, שולדור"ש בְּלַעַ"ז (רִתּוּךְ).


**Thin strips of metal.** Hammered, resembling the hammering of a thin metal plate. They did not protrude in its thickness nor were they engraved in a depression. And Yonoson rendered לויות מעשה מורד, “joined by welding,” *souldriz,* in O.F.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:1)


**וְאַרְבָּעָה אוֹפַנֵּי.** שְׁנַיִם לְפָנֶיהָ וּשְׁנַיִם לְאַחֲרֶיהָ, כְּדֶרֶךְ הָעֲגָלוֹת הַגְּדוֹלוֹת.


**Four [copper] wheels.** Two in the front and two in the back in the manner of large wagons.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:2)


**וְסַרְנֵי נְחשֶׁת.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: וְנִסְרִין דִּנְחַשׁ, הֵם הַנְּסָרִים הָעֶלְיוֹנִים מוּל הַמִּסְגְּרוֹת.


**And copper bars.** [Targum] Yonoson rendered ונסרין דנחש, “boards of copper,” they are the upper boards against the frames.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:3)


**סַרְנֵי.** כְּמוֹ נְסָרִים.


**Bars.** [סרני means] the same as נסרים [=boards].


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:4)


**וְאַרְבַּע פַּעֲמֹתָיו.** שֶׁל כַּן שֶׁעֲלֵיהֶם.


**And its four corners.** Of the base [=כן]which was above them [i.e., wheels].


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:5)


**כְּתֵפֹת לָהֶם.** בּוֹלְטוֹת לְמַעְלָה מִן הַנְּסָרִים בְּזָוִיּוֹת הַכַּן. וְתַחַת הַכִּיּוֹר הַיּוֹשֵׁב עַל הַכַּן, הָיוּ הַכְּתֵפוֹת יְצוּקוֹת (סְפָרִים אֲחֵרִים: לְלַמֵּד שֶׁמִּתּוֹךְ הַכַּן הָיוּ הַכְּתֵפוֹת יוֹצְאוֹת בִּיצִיקָה אַחַת, וְהַכְּתֵפוֹת מַחֲזִיקוֹת לַכִּיּוֹר שֶׁלֹּא יִפֹּל לְמַטָּה לְתוֹךְ הַכַּן).


**Had shoulders.** They protruded above the boards in the corners of the base. And under the basin which rested upon the base, the shoulders were molten. (Other texts: This teaches [us], that the shoulders came from the base in one pouring, and the shoulders supported the basin so that it should not fall down through the base.)


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:30:6)


**מֵעֵבֶר אִישׁ לֹיוֹת.** מִצַּד כָּל אֶחָד מִן הַכְּתֵפוֹת, הָיָה לֹיוֹת שֶׁל זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה מְצֻיָּרוֹת. מֵעֵבֶר אִישׁ. כְּמוֹ אִישׁ אֶל אָחִיו הָאֲמוּרִים בַּכְּרוּבִים.


**At the side of each were thin metal strips joined together.** At the side of each of the shoulders was a joining of a decorated male and female. מעבר איש [=one to another] means the same as “איש אל אחיו [=one to another],”⁴⁹ which is stated by the cherubim.

footnotes:
⁴⁹ Shemos 25:20.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:31:1)


**וּפִיהוּ מִבֵּית לַכֹּתֶרֶת וָמַעְלָה בָּאַמָּה.** פִּיהוּ שֶׁל כַּן, יוֹצֵא מִתּוֹךְ פִּיהוּ שֶׁל כּוֹתֶרֶת, וְעוֹלֶה לְמַעְלָה אַמָּה גֹּבַהּ, וְהַכּוֹתֶרֶת הִיא גַּג הַמְּכוֹנָה עָשׂוּי כְּמִין כּוֹבַע מְשֻׁפָּע מִכָּל צְדָדָיו, וּבְאֶמְצַע נִקְבַּעַת נֶקֶב עָגֹל אַמָּה וַחֲצִי אַמָּה רֹחַב בֶּעָגוֹל, וּסְבִיב אוֹתוֹ נֶקֶב כְּמִין הֶקֵּף מְחִצָּה, חֲצִי אַמָּה גֹּבַהּ סָבִיב, וְכָךְ מְפֹרָשׁ לְמַטָּה בָּעִנְיָן, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: וּבְרֹאשׁ הַמְּכוֹנָה חֲצִי אַמָּה קוֹמָה עָגֹל סָבִיב. וְהוּא קָרוּי 'פִּי הַכּוֹתֶרֶת', וְהַכַּן מֻנָּח עַל אוֹתוֹ הֶקֵּף, וְשׁוּלֵי הַכַּן תְּחוּבִין בְּתוֹךְ פִּי הַהֶקֵּף, וּלְמַעְלָה הֵימֶנּוּ הוּא בּוֹלֵט אַמָּה, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר כָּאן: וּפִיהוּ מִבַּיִת לַכּוֹתֶרֶת וּמַעְלָה בָּאַמָּה.


**And the rim of the vessel was inside the crown and rose above it an *amoh*.** The mouth of the base extends outward of the rim of the crown and goes upward an *amoh* high. The crown is the roof of the base, made like a hat, [i.e.,] sloping on all sides, and in the center a round hole was set, with a diameter that was one and one half *amohs*, and around that hole was the resemblance of an enclosure of a partition one half *amoh* high around. And below this is thus explained regarding this subject, as it is stated, “At the top of the base [was a frame] half an *amoh* high circular all around,”⁵⁰ and that is called “פי הכותרות [=the rim of the crown].” And the base rests on that enclosure, and the bottom of the base is inserted into the rim of the enclosure, and it protrudes one *amoh* above it. This is what is stated here, “And the rim of the vessel was inside the crown and rose above it an *amoh*.”

footnotes:
⁵⁰ Below v. 35.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:31:2)


**וּפִיהָ עָגֹל מַעֲשֵׂה כֵן.** וּפִיהָ שֶׁל כּוֹתֶרֶת עָגֹל, דֻּגְמַת כַּן הֶעָגֹל.


**The rim was round made like the base.** And the rim of the crown was round, a replica of the base which was round.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:31:3)


**אַמָּה וַחֲצִי הָאַמָּה.** רָחְבָּהּ בֶּעָגוֹל.


**One and a half *amohs*.** Its width in diameter.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:31:4)


**וְגַם עַל פִּיה מִקְלָעוֹת.** צוּרַת פְּרָחִים.


**There were engravings on the rim as well.** Designs of flowers.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:32:1)


**וְאַרְבַּעַת הָאוֹפַנִּים לְמִתַּחַת לַמִּסְגְּרוֹת.** שֶׁהַמִּסְגְּרוֹת מִיַּד אוֹפַן לְיַד אוֹפַן, כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי.


**The four wheels were underneath the frames.** Because the frames [reached] from the axle of one wheel to the axle of the other wheel, as I have explained.⁵¹

footnotes:
⁵¹ Above v. 28.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:32:2)


**וִידוֹת הָאוֹפַנִּים.** הוּא עֵץ הַתָּחוּב בָּאוֹפַנִּים, וְקוֹרִין איישי"ל (צִיר שֶׁל גַּלְגַּל).


**And the axles of the wheels.** That is the wood which is inserted into the wheels and is called, *essieu* in [O.F.]


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:32:3)


**בַּמְּכוֹנָה.** דְּבוּקִים מְצוּקִים בַּמְּכוֹנָה בִּיצִיקָה אַחַת.


**Were in the base.** The axles were joined and melted to the base in one pouring.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:33:1)


**כְּמַעֲשֵׂה אוֹפַן הַמֶּרְכָּבָה.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: כְּעוֹבַד גִּלְגְּלֵי מֶרְכָּבָה, אוֹפַן בְּתוֹךְ הָאוֹפַן שְׁתִי וָעֶרֶב, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר בִּיחֶזְקֵאל, בַּמֶּרְכָּבָה גָבוֹהַּ.


**Like the work of a chariot wheel.** [Targum] Yonoson rendered, “as the work of the wheels of a chariot,” a wheel within a wheel crosswise, as it is stated in Yechezkeil in [reference to] the “Heavenly Chariot.”⁵²

footnotes:
⁵² Yechezkeil 1:16.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:33:2)


**יְדוֹתָם.** איישי"ל (צִיר שֶׁל גַּלְגַּל).


**Their axles.** *Essieu* [in O.F.]


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:33:3)


**וְגַבֵּיהֶם.** בוייל"ש בְּלַעַ"ז (טַבּוּר הַגַּלְגַּל), הֵם הַנְּקָבִים.


**Their hubs.** *Bojjols* in O.F., they are the holes [for the axles].


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:33:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:33:4)


**וְחִשֻּׁקֵיהֶם.** הֵם עֲגֻלִּים סָבִיב, שֶׁהוּא קִשּׁוּרָן.


**Their rims.** They are the enclosures around, which bind them together.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:33:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:33:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:33:5)


**וְחִשֻּׁרֵיהֶם.** זְרוֹעוֹת הָאוֹפַנִּים, הַתְּחוּבִים מִגַּבֵּיהֶם לְחִשּׁוּקֵיהֶם, שֶׁקּוֹרִין ריי"ש בְּלַעַ"ז (חִישׁוּרִים).


**And their spokes.** The arms of the wheels which are attached from the holes of the wheel to the rims, which is called *rais*, in O.F.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:34:1)


**וְאַרְבַּע כְּתֵפוֹת.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי לְמַעְלָה, וְאַרְבַּע פַּעֲמוֹתָיו כְּתֵפוֹת לָהֶם, וְלֹא שְׁנָאָן כָּאן אֶלָּא לוֹמַר וּמִן הַמְּכוֹנָה כְּתֵפֶיהָ.


**There were four shoulders.** As I have explained above, “and its four corners had shoulders,”⁵³ and he did not mention it here only to say, “the shoulders were of the base itself.”⁵⁴

footnotes:
⁵³ Above v. 30.
⁵⁴ I.e., they were not made separately and then attached to it.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:35:1)


**חֲצִי הָאַמָּה קוֹמָה עָגֹל.** הִיא מְחִצָּה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי לְמַעְלָה, שֶׁהִיא סְבִיב הַנֶּקֶב וְהָאַמָּה מְרֻבַּעַת.


**Half an *amoh* high circular.** This is the partition, as I explained above,⁵⁵ which was around the opening and the [uppermost] *amoh* [of the crown] was square.

footnotes:
⁵⁵ Above v. 31.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:35:2)


**וְעַל רֹאשׁ הַמְּכוֹנָה יְדֹתֶיהָ.** הֵם סַרְנֵי הַנְּחשֶׁת הָאֲמוּרִים לְמַעְלָה.


**And on the top of the base its axles.** They are the copper bars [סרני הנחשת] mentioned above.⁵⁶

footnotes:
⁵⁶ Above v. 30.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:35:3)


**וּמִסְגְּרֹתֶיהָ מִמֶּנָּה.** מִסְגְּרוֹת הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת הוּצְקוּ עִמָּהּ, וְלֹא שֶׁחִבְּרָן לְאַחַר כֵּן.


**And its frames. They were all of one piece.** The lower frames were poured together with it; he did not attach them afterwards.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:36:1)


**וַיְפַתַּח עַל הַלֻּחֹת יְדֹתֶיהָ וְעַל מִסְגְּרֹתֶיהָ.** הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת עַל שְׁנֵיהֶם, פִּתַּח וְחָקַק כְּרוּבִים אֲרָיוֹת.


**He engraved on the plates, on its axles, and on its frames.** On the lower ones. On both of them, he engraved and inscribed cherubim, lions.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:36:2)


**כְּמַעַר אִישׁ וְלֹיוֹת.** פִּתַּח סָבִיב כְּזָכָר וּנְקֵבָה הַחֲבוּקִים בִּזְרוֹעוֹתֵיהֶן, כֵּן פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ.


**(And palm trees, this is not [found] in other texts.) Pairs attached to each other.** He engraved around the likeness of a male and female that are joined by their arms, thus our Rabbis explained this.⁵⁷

footnotes:
⁵⁷ See Maseches Yoma 54a.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:36:3)


**כְּמַעַר.** לְשׁוֹן אֲחִיזָה וְדִבּוּק, כְּזָכָר הַמְּעוּרֶה בַּנְּקֵבָה, וּפֵרוּשׁוֹ: אִישׁ וְלוֹיוֹתוֹ. וּלְפִי מַשְׁמָעוּתוֹ 'לוֹיוֹת' לְשׁוֹן 'דִּבּוּק', שולדור"א בְּלַעַ"ז (הַלְחָמָה, רִתּוּךְ), כְּמוֹ לוֹיוֹת הָיוּ שָׁם, עֲשׂוּיוֹת כִּמְעָרַת אִישׁ, וְהַמְּעָרֶה כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁנוּ.


**Attached.** An expression of attachment, as a male that is attached to a female, and its meaning is, “a man with his attachment.” According to its simple meaning, לויות is an expression of “attachment,” *soldore*, in O.F. There were attachments there made as the joining of a man, and the joining was in the manner we explained.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:37:1)


**מוּצָק אֶחָד.** כְּשֵׁם שֶׁיָּצַק אֶת הָרִאשׁוֹנָה, כֵּן יָצַק אֶת כֻּלָּם.


**All cast in the same manner.** As he cast the first [base], he similarly cast them all.⁵⁸

footnotes:
⁵⁸ I.e., they were all identical. Alternatively, each base was cast as a single piece.—Radak.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:37:2)


פירוש מבעל צורת הבית:**וזה מעשה המכונה.** עשה ארבעה אופני נחושת, שנאמר (פסוק ל): **וארבע אופני נחשת למכונה האחת** כצורת עגלה גדולה, וקומת האופן האחד, אמה וחצי האמה. ומעשה האופנים כמעשה אופן המרכבה, אופן בתוך אופן, למען יוכל להוליכם אל אשר יהיה הרוח והרצון להוליכם, שמה יוליכם מבלי שיצטרך להפכם אנה ואנה, והמרחק שמאופן לאופן ממזרח למערב, ארבע אמות, וכן מצפון לדרום שהיא מרובעת, לא ארוכה כעגלות שלנו, וזהו שאמר (פסוק כז): **ארבע באמה ארך המכונה האחת, וארבע באמה רחבה** ומקל עב וארוך נתחב בשני האופנים שממזרח למערב הצפוניים, וכן בשנים הדרומיים וכן כמו באותן האופנים שבתוך אלו, היה מקל נתון ונתחב בשנים מהם שמצפון לדרום המזרחיים, וכן בשנים המערביים, ואלו המקלות קראם הכתוב 'ידות', זהו שאמר וידותיהם. ופירוש וגביהם, הוא מרכזי האופנים שבתוכה סובבים המקלות הללו, ונקראים 'גב', לפי שהיא הגובה שבאופן, בשכבו על הארץ, וכן כל מרכז העיגול הוא הגובה שממנו, לפי שכל הצדדין נמשכין ממנו ופירוש 'וחשוקיהם', הם העיגולים החיצונים, שמקשר חישוריהם שהם העצים הדקים, כמין זרועות הנתחבים ונתונים מגב לחישוק, והוא מלשון וחשוקיהם כסף (שמות כז י), והוא הגלגל המגלגל על הארץ, ורגילין לעשות סביבו ברזל עגול, ונקרא בלשון המשנה בפרק י"א דכלים (משנה ג): סובב של גלגל. וכאן לא היה צריך לסובב, לפי שהוא כולו מנחושת 'ומסגרות', פירוש: כמין טבלאות עשוי להם על הידות לארבע רוחות בריבוע, וזהו שאמר (פסוק לא): **ומסגרותיהם מרובעות לא עגולות.** והם סוגרים העגלה מארבע רוחותיה, לפיכך נקראו מסגרות ועל המסגרות הללו נסרים מנחושת, קראם הכתוב 'סרני נחשת', והיא מלה מסורסת, ועליהם, כלומר: מבחוץ ולא על גבם, עשויה כמין שליבות סולם, ומבין שליבה לשליבה מסגרות, ועליהם צורת בקר וגו'. והיו הנסרים עולים מעל המסגרות עד כנגד קומת האופנים, ומשופעים והולכים ככובע וכמין כיסוי שבעגלות צבים, עד בואם כנגד קומת האופן, ושם נמשכין זה אל זה עד שמכסין אותו, זולת באמצעו למעלה אינן מכסין, אבל יש בהם נקב עגול, רוחב עיגולו אמה וחצי, והכיסוי הזה נקרא 'כותרת', והנקב שבו 'פי הכותרת', וזהו שאמר (שם) ופיה עגול מעשה כן אמה וחצי האמה וסביב זה הנקב, עולה מחיצה בגובה חצי אמה, וזהו שאמר (פסוק לה): **ובראש המכונה חצי אמה קומה עגול סביב.** ובתוך המחיצה הזאת, נכנס ונתחב הכן, שהוא כלי קבול המקבל הכיור כשהכיור תחוב בכן, והכן בתוך מחיצה זו שעל העגלה, והכן בתחתונה הוא עגול בחצי האמה, כשיעור גובה המחיצה שעל הנקב העגול שבמכונה למעלה, ועולה הכן על המחיצה הזאת אמה, וזה מה שאמר (פסוק לא): **ופיהו מבית לכותרת ומעלה באמה.** ומעתה יש לנו שלש אמות קומת המכונה, שהאופן אמה וחצי, והמחיצה העגולה חצי אמה, ואמה יוצא גובה הכן מעליה, והאמה הזאת היא מרובעת, ולה ארבע כתפות בארבע פינותיה, וזהו שאמר (פסוק לד): **וארבע כתפות אל וגו' המכונה האחת מן המכונה כתפיה.** לפי שהכיור הוא למטה בשפולו משופע שיכנוס לתוך הכן, וכדי שלא יפול אל תוכו יותר משיעור הנרצה, לכך נעשו אלו הכתיפות, שישאו ויסבלהו, עד כאן לשון צורת הבית:



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:38:1)


**וַיַּעַשׂ עֲשָׂרָה כִיֹּרוֹת.** כְּתִיב בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָמִים: אֶת מַעֲשֵׂה הָעֹלָה יָדִיחוּ בָם.


**He made ten [copper wash] basins.** It is written in Divrei Hayomim, “the parts of the burnt-offering they rinsed with them.”⁵⁹

footnotes:
⁵⁹ II Divrei Hayomim 4:6.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:39:1)


**עַל כֶּתֶף הַבַּיִת.** כְּנֶגֶד כֶּתֶף הַבַּיִת הַיְמָנִית.


**On the [right] side of the Beis Hamikdosh.** Against the right side of the Beis Hamikdosh.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:39:2)


**מִכֶּתֶף הַבַּיִת הַיְמָנִית.** מָשׁוּךְ מִצַּד דָּרוֹם לְצַד צָפוֹן, וְזֶה קֵדְמָה מוּל נֶגֶב, מִקְצוֹעַ מִזְרָחִית צְפוֹנִית, כְּנֶגֶד הָאֲוִיר שֶׁבֵּין כֹּתֶל צְפוֹנִי שֶׁל בַּיִת לְצַד כֹּתֶל הָעֲזָרָה, וְצַד צְפוֹנִי קָרוּי מִמּוּל נֶגֶב, מָשׁוּךְ וּמְרֻחָק מִן הַדָּרוֹם, וְאֵין 'מוּל' וּ'מִמּוּל' שָׁוִין בְּפִתָּרוֹן.


**[Away] from the right side of the Beis Hamikdosh.** Extended from the south side toward the north side. The following [explains], “קדמה מול נגב”: The northeast corner against the space which is between the northern wall of the Beis Hamikdosh and the wall of the court; and the northern wall is called “ממול נגב,” extended and removed far away from the south, and מול and ממול are not translated the same.⁶⁰

footnotes:
⁶⁰ I.e., ממול נגב means “opposite the south,” which is the north.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:40:1)


**וַיַּעַשׂ חִירוֹם וְגוֹ'.** וְאָמַר רַבִּי, הַכִּיֹּרוֹת הֵן הֵן סִירוֹת, כְּמָה דְּאַתְּ אָמַר: כְּכִיּוֹר אֵשׁ בְּעֵצִים; וְכֵן: וְהִכָּה בַכִּיּוֹר.


**And Chirom made, etc.** And Rebbi said, that the כיורות are one and the same as the סירות, as it is stated, “like a pan of fire burning wood,”⁶¹ and similarly, “and he thrust it into the pan.”⁶²

footnotes:
⁶¹ Zecharyah 12:6.
⁶² I Shmuel 2:14.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:40:2)


**הַכִּיֹּרוֹת.** שֶׁל נְחשֶׁת, לְדַשֵּׁן אֶת הַמִּזְבֵּחַ.


**The [wash] basins.** Of copper, used to remove the ashes from the altar.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:40:3)


**הַיָּעִים.** מַגְרֵפוֹת שֶׁל נְחשֶׁת, שֶׁקּוֹרִין ווידו"ל (יָעֶה, מַגְרֵפָה) בְּאַשְׁכְּנַז, לַחֲתוֹת בָּהֶן דֶּשֶׁן בְּתוֹךְ הַסִּירוֹת.


**The rakes.** Copper shovels which are called *vedil*, in German, [were used] to rake the ashes into the pots.



### Verse 42

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:42:1)


**שְׁנֵי טוּרִים רִמּוֹנִים.** מֵאָה רִמּוֹנִים בְּטוּר, חֲרוּזִים בְּשַׁרְשְׁרוֹת.


**Two rows of pomegranates.** One hundred pomegranates in each row hanging by chains.



### Verse 46

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:46:1)


**יְצָקָם.** הִתִּיכָם וְהִצִּיקָם כְּתַבְנִיתָם.


**[The king] cast them.** He melted them and poured them according to their form.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:46:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:46:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:46:2)


**בְּמַעֲבֵה הָאֲדָמָה.** תַּרְגּוּם: בְּעוֹבֵי גַּרְגִּשְׁתָּא.


**In the thick clay.** [Targum Yonoson rendered,] “in the thickness of the clay.”⁶³

footnotes:
⁶³ The clay of the Jordan Plain was of very good quality.—Radak.



### Verse 47

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:47:1)


**וַיַּנַּח שְׁלֹמֹה.** מִלִּשְׁקֹל אֶת מִשְׁקַל הַכֵּלִים הָאֵלֶּה מֵרֹב מְאֹד וְחָדַל לִסְפֹּר מִשְׁקָלָם.


**Shlomo gave up.** [He refrained] from determining the weight of these vessels because there were very many, and [therefore] he ceased to determine their weight.⁶⁴

footnotes:
⁶⁴ Targum renders וינח as “he hid [=ואצנע],” implying that Shlomo prepared more than was needed and put away the extra vessels for future use. II Divrei Hayomim 4:18 implies that Shlomo made more vessels than needed.



### Verse 49

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:49:1)


**חָמֵשׁ מִיָּמִין וְחָמֵשׁ מִשְּׂמֹאל.** אִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר חָמֵשׁ מִימִין הַפֶּתַח וְחָמֵשׁ מִשְּׂמֹאל הַפֶּתַח, אִם כֵּן מָצִינוּ מְנוֹרָה בַּצָּפוֹן, וְהַתּוֹרָה אָמְרָה: עַל יֶרֶךְ הַמִּשְׁכָּן תֵּימָנָה, אֶלָּא שֶׁל משֶׁה בָּאֶמְצַע, חָמֵשׁ מִימִינָהּ, וְחָמֵשׁ מִשְּׂמֹאלָהּ.


**Five on the right and five on the left.** It is impossible to say five were on the right side of the entrance and five were on the left of the entrance, if so, we find a candlestick on the north [side] and the Torah states, “on the south side of the Tabernacle.”⁶⁵ Therefore, Moshe’s [candlestick] was in the middle, five [candlesticks] on its right and five on its left.

footnotes:
⁶⁵ Bamidbar 3:29. The verse quoted by Rashi describes the location where the Kehas family encamped. It is not stated in reference to the candlestick. See Shemos 26:35 and 40:24 for the location of the candlestick.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:49:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:49:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:49:2)


**וְהַפֶּרַח.** שֶׁל מְנוֹרָה.


**And the flowers.** Of the candlestick.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:49:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:49:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:49:3)


**וְהַנֵּרֹת.** בָּזִיכִין שֶׁנּוֹתְנִין בָּהֶן הַשֶּׁמֶן וְהַפְּתִילָה.


**The lamps.** The cups into which the oil and wicks are put.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:49:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:49:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:49:4)


**וְהַמֶּלְקָחַיִם.** שֶׁמְּרִימִים בָּהֶן אֶת הַפְּתִילָה מִתּוֹךְ הַשֶּׁמֶן.


**And the tongs.** With which the wick is lifted out of the oil.



### Verse 50

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:50:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:1)


**וְהַסִּפּוֹת.** כְּלִי שֶׁל מִינֵי זֶמֶר הוּא, וְכֵן מְזַמְּרוֹת.


**The Sippos.** A type of musical instrument,⁶⁶ and similarly, “מזמרות.”

footnotes:
⁶⁶ Alternatively, ספות are pitchers.—Metzudas Tzion.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:50:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:2)


**וְהַמִּזְרָקוֹת.** לְקַבֵּל דָּם.


**The basins.** To receive the blood.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:50:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:3)


**וְהַכַּפּוֹת.** לְבָזִיכֵי לְבוֹנָה.


**The spoons.** As vessels for frankincense.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:50:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:4)


**וְהַמַּחְתּוֹת.** לַחֲתוֹת בָּהֶן תְּרוּמַת הַדֶּשֶׁן וְגֶחָלִים לְהוֹלִיךְ מִמִּזְבֵּחַ הַחִיצוֹן לַמִּזְבֵּחַ הַפְּנִימִי לְהַקְטִיר קְטֹרֶת.


**And the fire pans.** To remove the ashes and coals, and to carry them from the outer altar to the inner altar to burn incense.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:50:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:5)


**וְהַפֹּתוֹת.** מַפְתְּחוֹת, שָׁמַעְתִּי. שֶׁמְּפַתְּחִין בָּהֶם אֶת הַמַּנְעוּל.


**The keys.** Keys, I heard, with which to open [מפתחין] with them the locks.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:50:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:6)


**לְדַלְתוֹת הַבַּיִת.** שֶׁהוּא קֹדֶשׁ קָדָשִׁים.


**For the doors of the [inner] Beis Hamikdosh.** Which is the Holy of Holies.


###### Rashi on I Kings 7:50:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:50:7)


**לְדַלְתֵי הַבַּיִת.** שֶׁהוּא לַהֵיכָל, כָּל פִּתְחוֹתֵיהֶן הָיוּ זָהָב.


**For the doors of the Beis Hamikdosh.** Which is for the Sanctuary, all of their entrances were of gold.



### Verse 51

###### Rashi on I Kings 7:51:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:51:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/7:51:1)


**אֶת קָדְשֵׁי דָּוִד אָבִיו.** מַה שֶּׁנִּשְׁאַר מִכֶּסֶף וְזָהָב שֶׁהִקְדִּישׁ אָבִיו. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה: שֶׁלֹּא רָצָה שְׁלֹמֹה לָתֵת מֵאוֹתוֹ הֶקְדֵּשׁ לְבִנְיַן הַבַּיִת. וְשָׁמַעְתִּי מֵחַכְמֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹמְרִין: לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁסּוֹפוֹ לֵחָרֵב, שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים אוֹמְרִים, קָשָׁה יִרְאָתָם, שֶׁלָּקְחוּ נִקְמָתָם מִן הַבַּיִת, שֶׁנִּבְנָה מִן גְּזֵלוֹת וַחֲבָלוֹת שֶׁגָּזַל מֵהֶם דָּוִד. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: כָּךְ אָמַר שְׁלֹמֹה, רָעָב הָיָה בִּימֵי אָבִיו שָׁלשׁ שָׁנִים, שָׁנָה אַחַר שָׁנָה, וְהָיָה לוֹ לְבַזְבֵּז הַהֶקְדֵּשׁוֹת הַלָּלוּ, לְהַחֲיוֹת בָּהֶן עֲנִיֵּי יִשְׂרָאֵל.


**The sacred of his father Dovid.** That which remained of the silver and gold dedicated by his father. But the Midrash explains that Shlomo did not wish to use any of those dedicated materials for the construction of the Beis [Hamikdosh]. And I heard from [Torah] scholars of Yisroel who said, because Dovid knew eventually it would be destroyed; so that the idol worshiping [nations] should not say that their idols are mightier for they took their revenge upon the Beis [Hamikdosh] which was built of the plunder and destruction which Dovid plundered from them. And others say, Shlomo said that there was a famine in the days of his father for three consecutive years, and he should have squandered these dedicated materials to sustain Yisroel’s poor.



## Chapter 8


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:1:1)


**נְשִׂיאֵי הָאָבוֹת לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** שֶׁל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.


**The leaders of the paternal families of the Bnei Yisroel.** Of the children of Yisroel.¹ the children of Yisroel.”

footnotes:
¹ The literal translation is, “to


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:1:2)


**מֵעִיר דָּוִד.** שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ שָׁם דָּוִד כְּשֶׁהֱבִיאוֹ מִבֵּית עוֹבֵד אֱדוֹם.


**From the city of Dovid.** Dovid had placed it there when he brought it from the house of Oved Edom.²

footnotes:
² See II Shmuel 6:10-12, 17.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:2:1)


**בְּיֶרַח הָאֵתָנִים.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: בְּיַרְחָא דְּעַתִּיקַיָּא דְּקָרַן לֵהּ יַרְחָא קַדְמָאָה בְּחַגָּא וּכְעָן הוּא יַרְחָא שְׁבִיעָאָה, מִשֶּׁנִּתְּנָה תוֹרָה וּכְתִיב בְּנִיסָן שֶׁהוּא רֹאשׁ חֳדָשִׁים, נִקְרָא תִּשְׁרֵי שְׁבִיעִי.


**In the month *Eisonim*.** [Targum] Yonoson rendered, “In the month which people of the ancient times called the first month, on the festival, and at present it is the seventh month,” i.e., since the Torah was given, and it is written in the month of Nissan which it is the first of the months, [therefore,] Tishrei³ is called the seventh [month].⁴

footnotes:
³ Although the construction of the Beis Hamikdosh was completed in the eighth month of the previous year [see above 6:38], Shlomo postponed its dedication until the following Tishrei.
⁴ איתן means “mighty” and ירח האיתנים refers to the month of Tishrei which has the strongest spiritual effect for people to improve themselves because of the holidays celebrated during Tishrei—Ralbag. Alternatively, איתנים alludes to the Patriarchs. See Maseches Rosh Hashana 11a.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:4:1)


**וְאֶת אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד.** שֶׁעָשָׂה משֶׁה, וּגְנָזוֹ שְׁלֹמֹה מִשֶּׁנִּבְנָה בַּיִת רִאשׁוֹן, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בְּתוֹסֶפְתָּא דְּסוֹטָה (יג א).


**The Tent of Meeting.** Which Moshe made and Shlomo stored this away as soon as the first Beis [Hamikdosh] was built, as we have learned in the Tosefta of Maseches Sotah.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ 13:1


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:4:2)


**אֲשֶׁר בָּאֹהֶל.** אֲשֶׁר נָטָה דָּוִד לָאָרוֹן.


**That were in the Tent.** Which Dovid pitched for the Ark.⁶

footnotes:
⁶ See II Shmuel 6:17



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:6:1)


**כַּנְפֵי הַכְּרוּבִים.** אוֹתָן שֶׁעָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה הָעוֹמְדִים עַל רַגְלֵיהֶם בַּקַּרְקַע, וְאֵין אֵלּוּ כְּרוּבִים שֶׁל כַּפֹּרֶת שֶׁעַל הָאָרוֹן.


**The wings of the *cherubim*.** Those which Shlomo made, which are standing on their feet on the ground; and these are not the cherubim of the cover which was on the Ark.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:7:1)


**אֶל מְקוֹם הָאָרוֹן.** מִקִּיר אֶל קִיר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה בָּעִנְיָן.


**Over the place of the Ark.** From wall to wall as it is stated above⁷ regarding this subject.

footnotes:
⁷ I Melochim 6:27.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:8:1)


**וַיֵּרָאוּ רָאשֵׁי הַבַּדִּים.** בַּפָּרֹכֶת שֶׁכְּנֶגֶד הַפֶּתַח בָּאַמָּה טְרַקְסִין, יָכוֹל יִהְיוּ מְקָרְעִין בָּהּ וְיוֹצְאִין, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר וְלֹא יֵרָאוּ הַחוּצָה, הָא כֵּיצַד, דּוֹחֲקִין וּבוֹלְטִין כְּעֵין שְׁנֵי דַּדֵּי אִשָּׁה, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: בֵּין שָׁדַי יָלִין.


**The ends of the poles were seen.** In the curtain which was at the entrance in the “Amoh Troksin.” One might think they tore through it [i.e., the curtain] and protruded [on the other side], it states, “but they could not be seen from outside.” How is this possible? They were pushing and bulging⁸ long vs. Moshe’s poles which were 10 *amohs* in length. Shlomo made them longer to enable more Kohanim to participate in carrying the Ark.—Ralbag similar to two breasts of a woman, as the matter is stated, “He lies between my breasts.”⁹¹⁰¹¹

footnotes:
⁸ These were new poles made by Shlomo to replace the ones made by Moshe. Shlomo’s poles were 20 amohs
⁹ I.e., “the Divine Presence dwelt between the Holy Ark’s poles.”
¹⁰ Shir Hashirim 1:13.
¹¹ Maseches Yoma 54a.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:10:1)


**בְּצֵאת הַכֹּהֲנִים מִן הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** הַכֹּהֲנִים שֶׁנָּשְׂאוּ הָאָרוֹן בַּדְּבִיר, כְּשֶׁיָּצְאוּ שָׁרְתָה שְׁכִינָה בַּהֵיכָל.


**After the *Kohanim* went out of the Kodesh.** As the *Kohanim* who carried the Ark into the Holy of Holies were leaving, the Divine Presence immediately came to rest in the Sanctuary.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:12:1)


**אָז אָמַר שְׁלֹמֹה.** כְּשֶׁרָאָה הֶעָנָן, אָמַר: עַתָּה רוֹאֶה אֲנִי שֶׁהַשְּׁכִינָה שׁוֹרָה בַּבַּיִת שֶׁבָּנִיתִי, שֶׁהֲרֵי כָּךְ הִבְטִיחַ לָבֹא וְלִשְׁכֹּן בּוֹ מִתּוֹךְ עָנָן וַעֲרָפֶל, וְהֵיכָן אָמַר: כִּי בֶּעָנָן אֵרָאֶה עַל הַכַּפֹּרֶת, כָּךְ שְׁנוּיָה בַּסִּפְרִי.


**Then Shlomo declared.** When he saw the cloud, he said, “Now I see that the Divine Presence is in the house which I built, for thus did He promise to come and dwell in it from the midst of a cloud and thick darkness.” And where did He say [this promise]? “For in the cloud will I appear upon the Ark cover.”¹² Thus is this taught in Sifri.

footnotes:
¹² Vayikra 16:2.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:13:1)


**לְשִׁבְתְּךָ עוֹלָמִים.** שֶׁמִּשֶּׁנִּבְחֲרָה, לֹא הֻתָּרוּ הַבָּמוֹת, וְלֹא שָׁרְתָה שְׁכִינָה בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר.


**A dwelling place for You forever.** From when it was chosen it would no longer be permissible [to offer sacrifices] on improvised altars, and the Divine Presence no longer dwelled in any other place.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:15:1)


**אֲשֶׁר דִּבֶּר בְּפִיו.** שֶׁיִּתֵּן לוֹ בֵּן שֶׁיִּבְנֶה בַּיִת.


**Who spoke with His mouth.** That He would grant him a son who would build the Beis [Hamikdosh].¹³

footnotes:
¹³ “With His mouth,” refers to the “voice” of prophecy which was given through Noson, the prophet, and repeated directly to Dovid. See II Shmuel 7:12-17.


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:15:2)


**וּבְיָדוֹ מִלֵּא.** קִיֵּם אֶת דְּבָרוֹ בְּיָדוֹ הַטּוֹבָה.


**And fulfilled it with His hand.** He has fulfilled His word with His good power.¹⁴

footnotes:
¹⁴ The literal meaning of ובידו מלא is, “and fulfilled with His hand.” Here it refers to God’s omnipotence, that He alone is able to fulfill any promise He makes, the hand symbolizing strength.—Radak



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:16:1)


**מִן הַיּוֹם וְגוֹ'.** אֶת זֶה דִּבֵּר אֶל דָּוִד אָבִי.


**Since the day, etc.** This is what He spoke to my father Dovid.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:22:1)


**וַיַּעֲמֹד שְׁלֹמֹה לִפְנֵי מִזְבַּח ה'.** כְּתִיב: כִּי עָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה כִּיּוֹר נְחשֶׁת וַיִּתְּנֵהוּ בְּתוֹךְ הָעֲזָרָה וְגוֹ', וַיַּעֲמֹד עָלָיו וַיִּבְרַךְ עַל בִּרְכָּיו.


**And Shlomo stood before the Altar of Adonoy.** [As] it is written, “and Shlomo made a copper basin and had set it in the midst of the court, etc. and upon it he stood, and knelt on his knees.”¹⁵¹⁶

footnotes:
¹⁵ Even though a king of Yisroel is allowed to sit in the Beis Hamikdosh [See II Shmuel 7:18], Shlomo chose to stand.
¹⁶ II Divrei Hayomim 6:13.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:23:1)


**שֹׁמֵר הַבְּרִית.** מַמְתִּין וְגוֹנֵז לִשְׁמֹר הַבְּרִית וְהַחֶסֶד, לְקַיֵּם הַבְטָחָתוֹ.


**Preserver of the covenant.** He waits and stores away [reward] to keep the covenant and [is based on] mercy, to fulfill His promise.¹⁷

footnotes:
¹⁷ Which He made with our Patriarchs.—Radak



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:24:1)


**אֲשֶׁר שָׁמַרְתָּ.** לְשׁוֹן אֲמָנַת הַבְטָחָה.


**You preserved.** [שמרת is] a expression meaning the fulfillment of a promise.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:27:1)


**הַאֻמְנָם.** לְשׁוֹן תֵּימָהּ.


**Will.** [This is] an expression of wonder.


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:27:2)


**לֹא יְכַלְכְּלוּךָ.** לֹא יְכִילוּךָ.


**Cannot contain You.** Cannot contain You.¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁸ The root of יכלכלוך is “כול”, meaning to contain, as in, “each basin could hold [=יכיל],” in 7:38 above.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:31:1)


**אֲשֶׁר יֶחֶטָא אִישׁ לְרֵעֵהוּ.** בּוֹעֵל אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ.


**Should a person sin against his fellow man.** One who has intimate relations with a married woman.


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:31:2)


**וְנָשָׁא בוֹ אָלָה.** כְּשֵׁם שֶׁהַמַּיִם בּוֹדְקִין אוֹתָהּ, כָּךְ הַמַּיִם בּוֹדְקִין אוֹתוֹ.


**And is liable that a curse.** Just as the water examines her, so does the water examine him.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ Rashi explains אלה as “a curse,” i.e., the waters examines “him” i.e., the husband, as well. In this instance, אלה does not mean “oath”, because the husband of a Sotah is not subject to an oath.


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:31:3)


**וְנָשָׁא בוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן נוֹשֶׁה.


**And is liable.** An expression of a creditor.


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:31:4)


**וּבָא אָלָה לִפְנֵי מִזְבַּחֲךָ.** וְהֶעֱמִיד הַכֹּהֵן אֶת הָאִשָּׁה לִפְנֵי ה'.


**And the curse comes before your altar.** “And the *Kohein* shall stand the woman before Adonoy.”²⁰

footnotes:
²⁰ Bamidbar 5:18



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:32:1)


**לָתֵת דַּרְכּוֹ בְּרֹאשׁוֹ.** וְצָבְתָה בִטְנָהּ וְגוֹ'.


**To set his [evil] way upon his head.** “And her abdomen will swell, etc.”²¹²²

footnotes:
²¹ Ibid. v. 27.
²² Alternatively, if one swears falsely in the Beis Hamikdosh, his punishment should be swift and obvious, so that people recognize the unique level of sanctity of the Beis Hamikdosh, and they would perceive that the Beis Hamikdosh as God’s chosen place.—Radak


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:32:2)


**וּלְהַצְדִּיק צַדִּיק.** וְנִקְּתָה וְנִזְרְעָה זָרַע, כָּךְ נִדְרַשׁ בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא דְּסוֹטָה.


**And to justify the righteous one.** “Then she will be cleared, and shall bear seed.”²³ Thus it is expounded in the Tosefta of Maseches Sotah.²⁴

footnotes:
²³ Bamidbar 5:28.
²⁴ 1:3.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:33:1)


**וְהוֹדוּ אֶת שְׁמֶךָ.** שֶׁחַיָּב אָדָם לְבָרֵךְ עַל הָרָעָה.


**And praise Your Name.** For a person is obliged to bless God for the bad.²⁵

footnotes:
²⁵ Maseches Berachos 54a.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:35:1)


**כִּי תַעֲנֵם.** לְמַעַן תַּעֲנֵם, 'כִּי' מְשַׁמֵּשׁ לְשׁוֹן 'אֲשֶׁר'.


**For You to answer them.** So that You may answer them; “כי” is an expression of “so that.”“



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:37:1)


**יֵרָקוֹן.** שֶׁהַתְּבוּאָה אֵינָהּ עוֹלָה בַּקָּנֶה לַעֲשׂוֹת שִׁבֹּלֶת.


**Rot.** When the wheat does not grow up on the stalk to make an ear.²⁶

footnotes:
²⁶ You will hear and answer them even though they did not repent wholeheartedly, i.e., their repentance was not out of love.—Metzudas Dovid



### Verse 43

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:43:1)


**כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָא אֵלֶיךָ הַנָּכְרִי.** וּבְיִשְׂרָאֵל הוּא אוֹמֵר: וְנָתַתָּ לָאִישׁ כְּכָל דְּרָכָיו, לְפִי שֶׁיִּשְׂרָאֵל מַכִּיר בְּהַקָּבָּ"ה, וְיוֹדֵעַ שֶׁהַיְכֹלֶת בְּיָדוֹ, וְאִם אֵין תְּפִלָּתוֹ נִשְׁמַעַת, תּוֹלֶה אֶת הַדָּבָר בְּעַצְמוֹ וּבְחֶטְאוֹ, אֲבָל עוֹבֵד גִּלּוּלִים קוֹרֵא תִּגָּר, וְאוֹמֵר: בַּיִת שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ הוֹלֵךְ לְסוֹף הָעוֹלָם, נִתְיַגַּעְתִּי לְכַמָּה דְּרָכִים וּבָאתִי וְהִתְפַּלַּלְתִּי בּוֹ, וְלֹא מָצָאתִי בּוֹ מַמָּשׁ, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאֵין מַמָּשׁ בַּעֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים, לְפִיכָךְ כְּכָל אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָא אֵלֶיךָ הַנָּכְרִי, אֲבָל יִשְׂרָאֵל, אִם אַתָּה רוֹאֶה שֶׁהוּא מַשְׁחִית בְּמָמוֹנוֹ אֶת חֲבֵרוֹ, אַל תִּתֵּן לוֹ.


**According to all that the stranger calls out to You.** And relating to the Jew it states, “and give to each one according to all his ways,”²⁷ because the Jew recognizes the Holy One Blessed Is He, and he knows that He has the ability in His power [to answer]; and if his prayers are not heard, he will blame the matter on himself and an account of his sins. But the non-Jew complains and says, “a house [i.e., Beis Hamikdosh], whose fame reaches to the ends of the world, I have exhausted myself on many roads, and have come and prayed in it and found no substance in it, just as there is no substance in idol worship.” Therefore, “according to all that the stranger calls out to You,”²⁸ but a Jew, if You see that he uses his wealth to destroy his friend, do not give him.

footnotes:
²⁷ Alternatively, when the wheat does not reach its normal color but turns to a pale yellow.—Metzudas Tzion
²⁸ Above v. 39.



### Verse 44

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:44:1)


**דֶּרֶךְ הָעִיר.** פּוֹנִים לְצַד יְרוּשָׁלַיִם.


**By way of the city.** Facing towards Yerusholayim.



### Verse 45

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:45:1)


**וְעָשִׂיתָ מִשְׁפָּטָם.** נִקְמָתָם בְּאוֹיְבֵיהֶם.


**And render their judgment.** [I.e.,] their vengeance against their enemies.



### Verse 51

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:51:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:51:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:51:1)


**כּוּר הַבַּרְזֶל.** הַחֶרֶס שֶׁמְּזַקְּקִין בּוֹ זָהָב, קָרוּי כּוּר.


**The smelting pot of iron.** The earthenware²⁹ utensils used to purify gold of its impurities is called כור.³⁰

footnotes:
²⁹ Alternatively, give a non-Jew anything he asks for, as incentive that he turn to God with his prayers, and to publicize God’s Presence in the Beis Hamikdosh.—Ralbag
³⁰ The phrase “of iron” refers to the purpose of the pot, not the material of which it is made. The pot itself is earthenware.



### Verse 56

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:56:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:56:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:56:1)


**כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר דִּבֵּר.** וְהֵיכָן דִּבֵּר: וְהֵנִיחַ לָכֶם מִכָּל אוֹיְבֵיכֶם.


**Like all that He had said.** And where did He speak? “And He will grant you rest from all your enemies.”³¹

footnotes:
³¹ A crucible used for melting gold.—Metzudas Tzion The oppression suffered by the Bnei Yisroel in Egypt is likened to a furnace which purifies metal, because Bnei Yisroel underwent a process of spiritual purification.



### Verse 59

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:59:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:59:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:59:1)


**לַעֲשׂוֹת מִשְׁפַּט עַבְדּוֹ וּמִשְׁפַּט עַמּוֹ.** לִתְבֹּעַ עֶלְבּוֹנָם מִיַּד הָאוֹיֵב.


**To do justice to His servant and the justice of His people.** To avenge their humiliation from their enemy.³²

footnotes:
³² Devarim 12:10.



### Verse 64

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:64:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:64:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:64:1)


**אֶת תּוֹךְ הֶחָצֵר.** דְּבָרִים כִּכְתָבָן, דִּבְרֵי רַבִּי יְהוּדָה. קִדֵּשׁ אֶת רִצְפַּת הָעֲזָרָה בִּקְדֻשַּׁת מִזְבֵּחַ, לְהַקְטִיר עַל הָרִצְפָּה.


**The inside of the Courtyard.** These words are to be taken literally, according to Rabbi Yehudah; [i.e.,] he hallowed the floor of the court with the [same] sanctity of the Altar, [enabling one] to offer sacrifices on the floor.³³

footnotes:
³³ Alternatively, משפט means “needs,” and the translation of the verse is, “to provide the needs of His servant and the needs of His people.”—Metzudas Tzion


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:64:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:64:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:64:2)


**כִּי מִזְבַּח הַנְּחשֶׁת.** מִזְבַּח הָאֲבָנִים שֶׁעָשָׂה תַּחַת מִזְבַּח הַנְּחשֶׁת.


**For the copper altar.** The stone altar that he [Shlomo] constructed in lieu of the copper altar.³⁴

footnotes:
³⁴ Maseches Zevochim 59a.


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:64:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:64:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:64:3)


**קָטֹן מֵהָכִיל אֶת הָעוֹלָה וְאֶת הַמִּנְחָה.** שֶׁהִרְבּוּ לְהָבִיא. אָמַר לוֹ רַבִּי יוֹסִי: וַהֲלֹא כְּבָר נֶאֱמַר: אֶלֶף עוֹלוֹת יַעֲלֶה שְׁלֹמֹה עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ שֶׁעָשָׂה משֶׁה, וּכְשֶׁאַתָּה מַגִּיעַ לְחֶשְׁבּוֹן אַמּוֹת וּלְמִנְיַן עוֹלוֹת, זֶה שֶׁל אֲבָנִים גָּדוֹל מִשֶּׁל משֶׁה, שֶׁמִּזְבַּח משֶׁה מְקוֹם מַעֲרַכְתּוֹ אַמָּה עַל אַמָּה, וְזֶהוּ הָיָה מְקוֹם מַעֲרַכְתּוֹ עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע עַל עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע, הֲרֵי שֶׁל שְׁלֹמֹה חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת שִׁבְעִים וְשֵׁשׁ כְּשֶׁל משֶׁה, אִם כֵּן מַהוּ: קִדַּשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ אֶת תּוֹךְ הֶחָצֵר, שֶׁקָּבַע בּוֹ מִזְבֵּחַ שֶׁל אֲבָנִים מְחֻבָּר בָּרִצְפָּה.


**Too small to contain the burnt-offerings and the meal offerings.** For they brought many. Rabbi Yose said to him [Rabbi Yehudah], “Is it not already written, ‘Shlomo offered a thousand burnt-offerings upon that altar,’³⁵ which Moshe had made,” and when you compute the number of *amohs* and the number of burnt offerings, this one of stones was larger than Moshe’s, for on Moshe’s altar the place used for the sacrifice, was one *amoh* by one *amoh*, but this one’s place used for the sacrifice was twenty-four [*amohs*] by twenty-four [*amohs*]. Therefore Shlomo’s [altar] was 576 times as large as Moshe’s.³⁶ If so, what is the meaning of, “the king sanctified the middle of the Courtyard?” [It means] that he set the stone altar into it, firmly connected to the floor.³⁷

footnotes:
³⁵ According to Ralbag, the copper was primarily made of stone but was plated with copper.
³⁶ I Melochim 3:4.
³⁷ See Maseches Zevochim 59b.


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:64:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:64:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:64:4)


**קָטֹן מֵהָכִיל.** בְּשֶׁל משֶׁה הוּא אוֹמֵר, כְּאָדָם שֶׁאוֹמֵר לַחֲבֵרוֹ: נַנָּס פְּלוֹנִי וּפָסוּל לַעֲבוֹדָה.


**Was too small to contain.** He is referring to Moshe’s [altar], like a person says to his friend, “So and so is a dwarf,” [i.e.,] he is disqualified to perform the service.³⁸

footnotes:
³⁸ Ibid.



### Verse 65

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:65:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:65:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:65:1)


**מִלְּבוֹא חֲמָת.** שֶׁהִיא בִּצְפוֹנָהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל.


**[Stretching] from the entrance of Chamos.** Located in the north of Eretz Yisroel.


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:65:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:65:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:65:2)


**עַד נַחַל מִצְרַיִם.** שֶׁהִיא כְּנֶגְדּוֹ בִּדְרוֹמָהּ, בְּאֵלֶּה מַסְעֵי.


**To the Brook of Egypt.** Which is opposite it, in the south, [as is delineated] in [the section entitled], “These are the travels.”³⁹

footnotes:
³⁹ See Bamidbar 34:5, 8. There, Targum Yonoson Ben Uziel identifies the “Brook of Egypt” [מצרים נחלה] as the Nile River.


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:65:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:65:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:65:3)


**שִׁבְעַת יָמִים.** שֶׁל חִנּוּךְ.


**Seven days.** Of inauguration.


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:65:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:65:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:65:4)


**וְשִׁבְעַת יָמִים.** חַג הַסֻּכּוֹת, נִמְצָא שֶׁאָכְלוּ וְשָׁתוּ בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים.


**And seven [more] days.** Of Succos.⁴⁰ It is found that they ate and drank on Yom Kippur.⁴¹⁴²

footnotes:
⁴⁰ The Rabbis in Maseches Mo’ed Katan 9a, deduce from the redundancy of this phrase [“seven days and seven more days”], that one is not permitted to combine one שמחה with another, e.g., one is not allowed to celebrate a wedding on Chol Hamoed.
⁴¹ See Maseches Mo’ed Katan 9a and Rashi there.
⁴² They began to celebrate on the eighth of Tishrei and continued for the next fourteen days. Thus, the third day of the dedication was Yom Kippur, the tenth of Tishrei.



### Verse 66

###### Rashi on I Kings 8:66:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:66:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:66:1)


**לְדָוִד עַבְדּוֹ.** לְהוֹדִיעַ שֶׁמָּחַל לוֹ עֲוֹנוֹתָיו, כִּדְאִיתָא בְּמוֹעֵד קָטָן: בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁבִּקֵּשׁ שְׁלֹמֹה לְהַכְנִיס הָאָרוֹן לְבֵית קָדְשֵׁי הַקֳּדָשִׁים, דָּבְקוּ שְׁעָרִים זֶה בָּזֶה.


**For Dovid His servant.** To make known that He forgave them their sins, as we find in [Maseches] Mo’ed Katan.⁴³ When Shlomo wished to bring the Ark into the Holy of Holies, the gates clung to one another.

footnotes:
⁴³ 9a.


###### Rashi on I Kings 8:66:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:66:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/8:66:2)


**וּלְיִשְׂרָאֵל עַמּוֹ.** שֶׁמָּחַל לָהֶם עַל עֲוֹן יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים, וְיָצְתָה בַּת קוֹל וְאָמְרָה: כֻלְּכֶם מְזֻמָּנִים לְחַיֵּי הָעוֹלָם הַבָּא.


**And for Yisroel His people.** That He forgave them the sin of Yom Kippur,⁴⁴ and a *Bas* *Kol* emanated and declared, “All of you are prepared for the life of the World to Come.”⁴⁵

footnotes:
⁴⁴ I.e., for having eaten on Yom Kippur.
⁴⁵ Maseches Mo’ed Katan 9a.



## Chapter 9


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:3:1)


**וְהָיוּ עֵינַי וְלִבִּי.** תַּרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן: וּתְהִי שְׁכִנְתִּי שַׁרְיָא בֵיהּ אִם רְעוּתִי מִתְעַבְּדָא – יְהֵא עֵינַי שָׁם, אִם לִבִּי וְחֶפְצִי שָׁם.


**And My eyes and My heart will be.** [Targum] Yonoson rendered, “and My Divine Presence will dwell there if My will is done.” **And My eyes and My heart will be there.** If My heart and My will are there.¹²

footnotes:
¹ I.e., if My desire and My will is being followed, My Divine Presence will be there.
² Even after the Beis Hamikdosh was destroyed, the Divine Presence remained on the Western Wall [כותל המערבי] of the Beis Hamikdosh. See Shir Hashirim 2:9 and Midrash Rabboh there.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:7:1)


**וְאֶת הַבַּיִת אֲשֶׁר הִקְדַּשְׁתִּי לִשְׁמִי.** תְּנַאי הוּא בֵּינִי וּבֵינֵיכֶם, וְאִם לֹא תִּשְׁמְעוּ, מַה נֶּאֱמַר שָׁם: וַהֲשִׁמּוֹתִי אֶת מִקְדְּשֵׁיכֶם.


**And the House that I have sanctified for My Name.** There is a condition between Me and you, [that] “If you heed not heed, etc.,” what is stated there? “I will bring your Sanctuaries into desolation.”³

footnotes:
³ Vayikra 26:31.


###### Rashi on I Kings 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:7:2)


**וְלִשְׁנִינָה.** כְּתַרְגּוּם: וּלְשׁוֹעֵי, יְסַפְּרוּ אֶת הָרָעוֹת הַבָּאוֹת עָלָיו וְיִתְלַעֲגוּ בּוֹ, כְּמוֹ: וַיְסַפֵּר, וְאִשְׁתָּעֵי. וּלְשׁוֹן 'שְׁנִינָה' אַף הוּא לְשׁוֹן 'דִּבּוּר', דִּכְתִיב: וְשִׁנַּנְתָּם לְבָנֶיךָ.


**And a mockery.** As the Targum [Yonoson rendered], ולשועי, [i.e.,] they will tell about the evils that have befallen them and will scoff at them, as in, “ויספר [=and he told,” which the Targum rendered] ואשתעי. The expression “שנינה” is also an expression of speech as it is written [in Scriptures], “and you shall repeat them [ושננתם] to your children.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:8:1)


**וְהַבַּיִת הַזֶּה יִהְיֶה עֶלְיוֹן.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא חֲטָאתֶם, וּמִשֶּׁתֶּחֶטְאוּ, כָּל עוֹבֵר עָלָיו יִשּׁוֹם וְיִשְׁרֹק. וְכֵן כָּתוּב בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים: וְהַבַּיִת הַזֶּה אֲשֶׁר הָיָה עֶלְיוֹן כָּל עוֹבֵר עָלָיו וְגוֹ'. וְכֹה פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: וְהַבַּיִת הַזֶּה אֲשֶׁר הָיָה עֶלְיוֹן וְנוֹרָא אַף לְעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה: וְגַם אֶל הַנָּכְרִי אֲשֶׁר לֹא מֵעַמְּךָ וְגוֹ', וְעַכְשָׁיו בְּחֻרְבָּנוֹ, כָּל הָעוֹבֵר עָלָיו, אַף עוֹבֵד כּוֹכָבִים, יִשּׁוֹם וְיִשְׁרֹק, וְיֹאמַר כִּי חֶטְאוֹ שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל גּוֹרֵם.


**And this Beis Hamikdosh will be most high.** So long as you have not sinned; but once you sin, every passerby will be astounded and will hiss.⁴ And similarly it is written in Divrei Hayomim, “And this Beis [Hamikdosh] that was exalted, all who pass by it, etc.,”⁵ and this is its interpretation, “and this house that was exalted and revered even by the gentiles,” as is stated above, “And even to the stranger who is not of Your people, etc.,”⁶ But, now in its destruction, everyone who passes by it, even the gentiles, will be astounded and hiss, and will say that the Jews’ sins were the cause.

footnotes:
⁴ Rashi understands that יהיה is the future tense, meaning “will be” most high. Alternatively, יהיה can also be interpreted in the present tense, “is” most high—Ralbag. Or, יהיה עליון is interpreted as “will be destroyed.”—Radak
⁵ II Divrei Hayomim 7:41.
⁶ I Melochim 8:21.


###### Rashi on I Kings 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:8:2)


**יִשֹּׁם.** יִתְמַהּ, כְּמוֹ: וְשָׁמְמוּ עָלֶיהָ אוֹיְבֵיכֶם; עַל יוֹמוֹ נָשַׁמּוּ אַחֲרוֹנִים.


**Will be astounded.** [ישם means] “will wonder,” as in, “and your enemies will be astonished [ושממו] about it,”⁷ [and as in,] “Concerning his day, the later ones will wonder [נשמו].”⁸

footnotes:
⁷ Vayikra 26:32.
⁸ Iyov 18:20.


###### Rashi on I Kings 9:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:8:3)


**וְשָׁרָק.** שופילי"ר בְּלַעַ"ז (לִשְׁרוֹק), וְכֵן דֶּרֶךְ כָּל הָרוֹאֶה חֻרְבָּן פִּתְאֹם, לִשְׁרֹק.


**And whistle.** *Siffler*, in O.F. The habit of anyone who suddenly sees desolation, is to hiss.⁹

footnotes:
⁹ When people are amazed at what they see, they often whistle.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:10:1)


**עֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה.** שֶׁבַע שָׁנִים לְבֵית ה', וְאֶת בֵּיתוֹ שְׁלשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה.


**Twenty years.** I.e., seven years [to build] the Beis Hamikdosh, and thirteen years [to build] his own palace, as mentioned above.¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ Above 6:38, and 7:1.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:11:1)


**נִשָּׂא אֶת שְׁלֹמֹה.** עִנְיַן עֹמֶס, כִּלְכֵּל אֶת מַשָּׂאוֹ בְּדָבָר זֶה.


**Provided Shlomo.** An expression of bearing, [i.e.,] he bore his burden in this matter.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:13:1)


**אֶרֶץ כָּבוּל.** אֶרֶץ כְּבָלִים, אֶרֶץ טִיט, שֶׁהָרֶגֶל מִשְׁתַּקֵעַ וְנִכְבֶּלֶת בָּהּ.


**The land of Kovul.** A land of chains, [i.e.,] a marshland into which the foot sinks and is entrapped.¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ It was as troublesome to walk on, as it is difficult for a man who is chained in shackles to walk, i.e., the foot is trapped as if it were in a chain [=כבל]—Radak. The land was not fertile. Alternatively, the people of כבל wore chains of silver and gold, i.e., they were wealthy and spoiled, and Chirom did not want to deal with them. See Maseches Shabbos 54a and Rashi there.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:15:1)


**וְזֶה דְבַר הַמַּס.** מַה שֶּׁמְּפָרֵשׁ בְּסוֹף הָעִנְיָן: כָּל הָעָם הַנּוֹתָר וְגוֹ', וַיַּעֲלֵם שְׁלֹמֹה לְמַס עוֹבֵד, אוֹתוֹ הַמַּס הָיָה לִבְנוֹת בּוֹ אֶת כָּל אֵלֶּה.


**And this is the review of the tax.** Described at the end of this topic, “All the people that remained, etc.,¹² Shlomo levied a tax of labor.”¹³ The tax levy was to build all these [structures].

footnotes:
¹² Below v. 20.
¹³ Below v. 21.


###### Rashi on I Kings 9:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:15:2)


**וְאֶת הַמִּלּוֹא.** מָקוֹם הָיָה בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם בְּעִיר דָּוִד, וְנִקְרָא מִלּוֹא, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁמֻּקָּף חוֹמָה נְמוּכָה, וּמִלְּאוּ לְתוֹכָהּ עָפָר.


**The Millo.** [Millo was] a place in Yerusholayim, in the City of Dovid, that was called Millo, because it was enclosed by a low wall and it was filled [ומלאו] with dirt.¹⁴

footnotes:
¹⁴ Alternatively, it was a large open plaza without any buildings, where people gathered [=מלא]—Ralbag.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:16:1)


**שִׁלֻּחִים.** נְדוּנְיָא.


**Present.** A dowry.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:18:1)


**וְאֶת תַּדְמוֹר בַּמִּדְבָּר בָּאָרֶץ.** אֵצֶל הַיִּשּׁוּב.


**And Tadmor in the desert of the land.** [I.e., it was located] near a settlement.¹⁵

footnotes:
¹⁵ The expression במדבר בארץ [=in the desert in the land] is somewhat ambiguous. Therefore Rashi suggests that the desert was located near a civilized area. Alternatively, the city was bordered by a desert on one side, and by arable land on the other side.—Ralbag



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:19:1)


**עָרֵי הַמִּסְכְּנוֹת.** (תרגום:) קִרְוֵי בֵּית אוֹצָרַיָּא.


**The store cities.** [Targum Yonoson rendered,] “cities used for storage.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:23:1)


**שָׂרֵי הַנִּצָּבִים.** מְמֻנִּים עַל הַמְמֻנִּים.


**Supervising officers.** Commanders over the officers.


###### Rashi on I Kings 9:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:23:2)


**חֲמִשִּׁים וַחֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת.** שְׁלשׁ מֵאוֹת מֵהֶם הָיוּ גֵּרִים הַמְמֻנִּים עַל שִׁבְעִים אֶלֶף נוֹשֵׂא סַבָּל וּשְׁמוֹנִים אֶלֶף חוֹצֵב בָּהָר, וּשְׁלשֶׁת אֲלָפִים וּשְׁלשׁ מֵאוֹת הָרוֹדִים בָּעָם, שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה: לְבַד מִשָּׂרֵי הַנִּצָּבִים לִשְׁלֹמֹה וְגוֹ' שְׁלשֶׁת אֲלָפִים וּשְׁלשׁ מֵאוֹת. וּבְדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים הוּא אוֹמֵר: שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת, אֵלּוּ שְׁלשׁ מֵאוֹת שֶׁחָסֵר שָׁם, מָנָה כָּאן, שֶׁהָיוּ מְמֻנִּים עַל כֻּלָּן, וּמָאתַיִם וַחֲמִשִּׁים הַנּוֹתָרִים, הָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל, מְמֻנִּים עַל שְׁאָר עוֹשֵׂי הַמְּלָאכָה. וּבְדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים מְנָאָן לְעַצְמָן: וְאֵלֶּה שָׂרֵי הַנִּצָּבִים לִשְׁלֹמֹה חֲמִשִּׁים וּמָאתַיִם הָרֹדִים בָּעָם, עוֹשֵׂי הַמְּלָאכָה.


**Five hundred fifty.** Three hundred of them were proselytes in charge of 70,000 who bore burdens, 80,000 hewers in the mountains, and 3,300 who controlled the people who did the work, as stated above, “These were in addition to Shlomo’s chief officers, etc., three thousand three hundred [officers].” But in Divrei Hayomim it states, “six hundred.”¹⁶ These three hundred that are missing here, he counted there, for they were appointed over all of them. The two hundred fifty remaining were Bnei Yisroel appointed over the other laborers. In Divrei Hayomim¹⁷ they were counted separately, [as it states,] “and these were Shlomo’s supervising officers, two hundred fifty, who controlled “the people who did the work,” the laborers.

footnotes:
¹⁶ II Divrei Hayomim 2:17.
¹⁷ Divrei Hayomim 8:10.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:24:1)


**אַךְ בַּת פַּרְעֹה.** שֶׁהָיְתָה מִתְּחִלָּה בְּעִיר דָּוִד, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּרֹאשׁ הַסֵּפֶר: וַיְבִיאָהּ אֶל עִיר דָּוִד.


**Only then did the daughter of Pharaoh.** Who was originally in the city of Dovid, as it is stated in the beginning of the book, “and he brought her to the City of Dovid.”¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁸ Above 3:1.


###### Rashi on I Kings 9:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:24:2)


**עָלְתָה מֵעִיר דָּוִד וְגוֹ'.** וּבְדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים מְפָרֵשׁ: כִּי אָמַר לֹא תֵשֵׁב אִשָּׁה לִי בְּעִיר דָּוִד כִּי קֹדֶשׁ הֵמָּה אֲשֶׁר בָּאָה אֲלֵיהֶם אֲרוֹן ה'.


**Came up from the city of Dovid, etc.** In Divrei Hayomim it explains, “for he said, ‘I should not have a wife live with me in [the City of] Dovid because they are holy [places], for the Ark of Adonoy was brought there.’”¹⁹ of Dovid.”

footnotes:
¹⁹ II Divrei Hayomim 8:11. The text there reads, “in the house


###### Rashi on I Kings 9:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:24:3)


**אָז בָּנָה.** שְׁלֹמֹה.


**Then he built up.** [I.e.,] Shlomo.


###### Rashi on I Kings 9:24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:24:4)


**אֶת הַמִּלּוֹא.** לִבְנוֹת לְתוֹכוֹ בָּתִּים, לַעֲבָדֶיהָ וּלְשִׁפְחוֹתֶיהָ, וְזֶה שֶׁהוֹכִיחוֹ יָרָבְעָם: אָבִיךָ הִנִּיחוֹ פָּרוּץ לְעוֹלֵי רְגָלִים, וְאַתָּה בָּנִיתָ אוֹתוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת אַנְגַּרְיָא לְבַת פַּרְעֹה, וְזֶהוּ 'אַךְ' הָאָמוּר כָּאן, אַךְ בְּזוֹ הֶעֱוָה שְׁלֹמֹה, שֶׁבָּנָה לָהּ אֶת הַמִּלּוֹא, כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי. וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר, אַךְ בַּת פַּרְעֹה וְגוֹ', כְּלוֹמַר הֶעָרִים הָאֲמוּרוֹת לְמַעְלָה, הֻצְרַךְ לִבְנוֹת לְמִסְכְּנוֹת לָרֶכֶב וְלַפָּרָשִׁים וְלַגְּדֻלָּה, אַךְ אֶת הַמִּלּוֹא, לֹא הָיָה בּוֹנֶה לְשׁוּם גְּדֻלָּה, כִּי אָבִיו הִנִּיחוֹ לִפְרֹשׂ בּוֹ עוֹלֵי רְגָלִים אֹהָלִים, אֶלָּא שֶׁבַּת פַּרְעֹה עָלְתָה אֶל בֵּיתָהּ, וְהַמִּלּוֹא סָמוּךְ לְאוֹתוֹ בַּיִת, אָז בָּנָה אֶת הַמִּלּוֹא.


**The Millo.** To build within it houses for her menservants and maidservants. Concerning this Yerovom admonished him [saying], “Your father left it open for the festival pilgrims, and you closed it up to make a labor force for Pharaoh’s daughter.”²⁰ And this “but” mentioned here [means,] but in this Shlomo sinned, that he had built for her the Millo. So have I heard, but I say, “But then did the daughter of Pharaoh, etc.,” I.e., the above mentioned cities were necessary to build for store cities, chariots, riders, and greatness; but, the Millo he did not build for any greatness, for his father had left it for the festival pilgrims to pitch their tents there, but since Pharaoh’s daughter had gone up to her house, and the Millo was adjacent to that house, then he built up the Millo.²¹

footnotes:
²⁰ Paraphrasing below 11:27. See Maseches Sanhedrin 101b and Rashi there.
²¹ Alternatively, the house of Pharaoh’s daughter was built on the area of the Millo.—Radak



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:25:1)


**עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** שֶׁל בֵּית עוֹלָמִים.


**On the altar.** I.e., the altar of the Beis Hamikdosh.


###### Rashi on I Kings 9:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:25:2)


**וְהִקְטִיר אֹתוֹ.** וְאוֹתוֹ מִזְבַּח הַקְּטֹרֶת אֲשֶׁר לִפְנֵי ה' לִקְטֹרֶת הַסַּמִּים.


**And he burnt incense [offerings] with it.** And [he burnt the incense on] the incense altar that was before God for the burning of incense.²²

footnotes:
²² See Shemos 30:1-10.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on I Kings 9:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/9:26:1)


**וָאֳנִי.** אֳנִיָּה.


**A ship.** A ship.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Kings 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:5:1)


**וְעֹלָתוֹ אֲשֶׁר יַעֲלֶה בֵּית ה'.** דֶּרֶךְ מָבוֹא שֶׁהֵכִין מִבֵּיתוֹ לְבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, שֶׁהָיָה עוֹלֶה בוֹ לְבֵית ה'.


**And his elevated ramp by which he would go up to the Beis Hamikdosh of Adonoy.** By means of a passageway that he had prepared from his house to the Beis Hamikdosh, by which he would go up to the House of God.¹

footnotes:
¹ Alternatively, ועולתו אשר יעלה עליו refers to the sacrifices that Shlomo offered in the Beis Hamikdosh, described above in 9:25.—Targum



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Kings 10:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:11:1)


**עֲצֵי אַלְמֻגִּים.** קוריי"ל בְּלַעַ"ז (אַלְמוֹגִים).


**Coral.** *Corail*, in O.F.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Kings 10:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:12:1)


**מִסְעָד לְבֵית ה'.** וּבְדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים כְּתִיב: מְסִלּוֹת לְבֵית ה', לְכָךְ אֲנִי אוֹמֵר, הַמִּסְעָד הַזֶּה לְשׁוֹן רִצְפָּה.


**A pathway to the Beis Hamikdosh of Adonoy.** But in Divrei Hayomim it is written, “paths [מסילות] to the House of Adonoy.”² I therefore say, that this support [מסעד] is an expression of a pavement.

footnotes:
² II Divrei Hayomim 9:11.


###### Rashi on I Kings 10:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:12:2)


**לַשָּׁרִים.** לִבְנֵי לֵוִי.


**For the singers.** For the *Leviyim*.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Kings 10:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:13:1)


**נָתַן לְמַלְכַּת שְׁבָא.** נְתִינָה זוֹ אֵינָהּ אֶלָּא לִמּוּד חָכְמָה.


**Gave to the queen of Sheva.** This giving refers only to the teaching of wisdom.


###### Rashi on I Kings 10:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:13:2)


**כָּל חֶפְצָהּ.** (בָּא אֵלֶיהָ וְנוֹלַד מִמֶּנָּה נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, וְהֶחֱרִיב הַבַּיִת שֶׁעָמַד אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְעֶשֶׂר שָׁנִים בְּחֵלֶק כָּל שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר שְׁבָטִים, מֵהָאֲרִ"י זַ"ל). מִלְּבַד אֲשֶׁר נָתַן לָהּ. מַתָּנוֹת וּמִגְדָּנוֹת הַמְּצוּיִין כָּאן וְאֵינָם מְצוּיִין בִּמְקוֹמָהּ.


**All that she desired.** (He had relations with her and Nevuchadnetzar was born, and he later destroyed the Beis [Hamikdosh] that had stood 410 years in the territory of all the twelve tribes. Rabbi Yitzchok [Luria] of blessed memory) [He gave her more,] in addition to giving her gifts and delicacies that are found here but are not found in her place.³

footnotes:
³ Alternatively, Shlomo gave her gifts of intellectual wisdom by answering all the problems and questions that she had asked, in addition to solving the riddles that she had posed to him.—Ralbag



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on I Kings 10:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:14:1)


**שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת שִׁשִּׁים וָשֵׁשׁ.** חִירָם נָתַן לוֹ מֵאָה וְעֶשְׂרִים, וּמַלְכַּת שְׁבָא מֵאָה וְעֶשְׂרִים, וָאֳנִי תַּרְשִׁישׁ הֵבִיאָה מֵאוֹפִיר אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְעֶשְׂרִים, הֲרֵי שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת וְשִׁשִּׁים, וְהַשִּׁשָּׁה לֹא יָדַעְתִּי מֵאַיִן. וּבְדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים כְּתִיב: שֶׁהֵבִיאוּ מֵאוֹפִיר אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וַחֲמִשִּׁים, וְאֵין מוֹנֶה שָׁם מֵאָה וְעֶשְׂרִים שֶׁל חִירָם, וְיֵשׁ לְתַקֵּן הַמִּקְרָא, שֶׁהַשְּׁלשִׁים הַיְתֵרִים הָיוּ מִן הַמֵּאָה וְעֶשְׂרִים שֶׁל חִירָם, שֶׁהֲרֵי עַבְדֵי חִירָם הָיוּ שָׁם עִם עֲבָדָיו.


**Six hundred sixty-six.** Chirom gave him 120 [talents], the Queen of Sheva 120, the ships of Tarshish brought from Ophir 420, totaling 660 [talents]. I do not know from where the other six came. It is written in Divrei Hayomim that [the ships brought] from Ophir 450 [talents],⁴ however it does not include the 120 talents of Chirom. We can resolve the contradictions that the extra 30 were from the 120 of Chirom, because Chirom’s servants were in Ophir with his servants.

footnotes:
⁴ See II Divrei Hayomim 8:18.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on I Kings 10:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:15:1)


**לְבַד מֵאַנְשֵׁי הַתָּרִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְבִיאִים לוֹ הַסּוֹחֲרִים הַתָּרִים בָּאָרֶץ לִסְחוֹרָה.


**This was in addition [to the gold that came from] traveling merchants.** Merchants would bring him who visited the land on business.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ Alternatively, retailers who sell small quantities, spy [=תרים] out the land to determine where to do business.—Metzudas Tzion


###### Rashi on I Kings 10:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:15:2)


**הָרֹכְלִים.** מוֹכְרֵי בְּשָׂמִים, וּמוֹלִיכִין אוֹתָם מֵעִיר לְעִיר.


**Spice dealers.** Spice dealers who would carry spices from one city to another.


###### Rashi on I Kings 10:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:15:3)


**וְכָל מַלְכֵי הָעֶרֶב.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: וְכָל מַלְכֵי סוּמְכְוָתָא, לְשׁוֹן עֲרֻבָּה, הַמְּלָכִים שֶׁהָיוּ בַעֲרֻבָּתָן וְסוֹמְכִין עָלָיו, גאראנטיא"ה בְּלַעַ"ז (מִלְשׁוֹן עַרְבוּת).


**And all the subordinate kings.** [Targum] Yonoson rendered, “and all dependent upon his support,”⁶ an expression of guarantee, [i.e.,] kings who were allies and were dependent upon his support, *garantie*, in O.F.

footnotes:
⁶ Alternatively, Arab kings.—Metzudas Tzion



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Kings 10:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:16:1)


**צִנָּה.** מַקֶּפֶת אֶת הָאָדָם מִשְּׁלשָׁה רוּחוֹתָיו, וְהוּא שֶׁאָמַר בְּסֵפֶר תְּהִלִּים: כַּצִּנָּה רָצוֹן תַעְטְרֶנּוּ.


**Full shields.** [A body shield that] encircles the person on three sides. This is the meaning of the verse in the Book of Tehillim, “as a body shield, with good, will You shall surround him.”⁷

footnotes:
⁷ Tehillim 5:13.


###### Rashi on I Kings 10:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:16:2)


**שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת זָהָב.** וְהוּא שֵׁשׁ מָנִים, שֶׁהַזָּהָב הוּא הַדִּינָר.


**Six hundred.** Six *manim*, for [a piece of] gold is a dinar.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Kings 10:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Kings/r/10:17:1)


**זָהָב שָׁחוּט.** הוּא רַךְ וְנוֹחַ לְהֵרָקַע.


**Soft gold.** It is soft and easy to flatten out.⁸
